only been established online during the previous year. Back then most people in the UK were betting at high street betting shops such as <a href="/William_Hill_Free_Bet.html">William Hill</a>. Online Betting Guide became more popularly known as OLBG, The Sports Betting Community.</p> <p>Whilst the most popular UK betting sports of <a href="https://www.olbg.com/betting-tips/Football/1">Football</a><span> </span>and <a href="https://www.olbg.com/betting-tips/Horse_Racing/2">Horse Racing</a><span> </span>have always been well provided for, OLBG has grown to cover more than 20 sports, hundreds of leagues and thousands of events. More than 43,000 <a href="https://www.olbg.com/tipster-competition">tipster competition</a><span> </span>prizes have been won, with over £650,000 of prizes shared.
